technologies = {

	fra_fighter_1 = {	# L 46

		allow_branch = {
			NOT = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
		}

		enable_equipments = {
			fra_fighter_equipment_1
		}

		path = {
			leads_to_tech = fra_fighter_2
			research_cost_coeff = 1
		}

		path = {
			leads_to_tech = fra_cas_1
			research_cost_coeff = 1
		}

		research_cost = 2.5
		start_year = 1934
		folder = {
			name = fra_air_techs_folder
			position = { x = 1 y = 2 }
		}

		categories = {
			major_technologies
			light_air
			cat_fighter
			air_equipment
		}

		sub_technologies = {
			fra_cv_fighter_1
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						WA_AI_RESEARCH_needs_attackers = yes
						WA_AI_RESEARCH_needs_cas = yes
						WA_AI_RESEARCH_needs_fighters = yes
						WA_AI_RESEARCH_needs_heavy_fighters = yes
						WA_AI_RESEARCH_needs_jet_fighters = yes
					}
				}
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_major_technologies > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_light_air > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_cat_fighter > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_air_equipment > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_t_fra_fighter_1 > 0 }
						}
					}
					date < 1933.1.1
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_major_technologies > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_light_air > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_cat_fighter > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_air_equipment > 0 }
							check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_t_fra_fighter_1 > 0 }
						}
					}
					date < 1932.1.1
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = WA_AI_unused_research_slots }
						date < 1934.1.1
					}
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = WA_AI_unused_research_slots
						date < 1933.1.1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		on_research_complete = {
			# WA_RB_CONSUME begin - generated by tools/gen/gen_research_bonus_tracking.py, do not edit
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_t_fra_fighter_1 > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { WA_rb_uses_t_fra_fighter_1 = 1 } if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_t_fra_fighter_1 < 1 } } clear_variable = WA_rb_uses_t_fra_fighter_1 } if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_t_fra_fighter_1 > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_t_fra_fighter_1 = 1 } if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_t_fra_fighter_1 < 1 } } clear_variable = WA_rb_ahead2_t_fra_fighter_1 } } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_major_technologies > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { WA_rb_uses_major_technologies = 1 } if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_major_technologies < 1 } } clear_variable = WA_rb_uses_major_technologies } if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_major_technologies > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_major_technologies = 1 } if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_major_technologies < 1 } } clear_variable = WA_rb_ahead2_major_technologies } } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_light_air > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { WA_rb_uses_light_air = 1 } if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_light_air < 1 } } clear_variable = WA_rb_uses_light_air } if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_light_air > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_light_air = 1 } if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_light_air < 1 } } clear_variable = WA_rb_ahead2_light_air } } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_cat_fighter > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { WA_rb_uses_cat_fighter = 1 } if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_uses_cat_fighter < 1 } } clear_variable = WA_rb_uses_cat_fighter } if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_cat_fighter > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_cat_fighter = 1 } if = { limit = { check_variable = { WA_rb_ahead2_cat_fighter < 1 } } clear_variable = WA_rb_ahead2_cat_fighter } } }
